## Providers

| Provider | Package | Key env | Model env | Default model | Role |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| OpenAI | `@ai-sdk/openai` | `OPENAI_API_KEY` | `OPENAI_MODEL` / `CARD_RECOGNITION_MODEL` | `gpt-6.1-sol` | Default for recognition and pricing |
| Claude | `@ai-sdk/anthropic` | `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` | `claude-sonnet-4-5` | Opt-in |
| Grok | `@ai-sdk/xai` | `XAI_API_KEY` | `XAI_MODEL` | `grok-4` | Opt-in |
| Gemini | `@ai-sdk/google` (4.0.105) | `GOOGLE_GENERATIVE_AI_API_KEY` | `GOOGLE_MODEL` | `gemini-2.5-flash` | Opt-in |

Routing precedence: explicit argument > per-task env (`CARD_RECOGNITION_PROVIDER` /
`CARD_PRICING_PROVIDER`) > `AI_PROVIDER` > `openai`. Implementation: `lib/ai-providers.ts`.
